The wall's reverse studio surface opens fully at the tilted base, the deliberate neglect spilling forward: unstretched canvases, the face-down school portrait now visible as a halftone blur, a jar of dried medium with its lid beside it. The paired motif, two identical price lists, has escaped the wall edges entirely; one lies flat on the white ground near her hip, unfolded to expose the dealer's markup column in archive red halftone; the other is crumpled in her right hand, the hand itself ordinary in scale, the fingers tight around the paper. Four spot colors reassign for this variation: archive red dominates the markup column and crumpled list_interior, chrome silver coats the raised mule and collapsed track, bone matte traces the splayed boning channels, and sour institutional green pools in the tilted wall's shadow and spilled studio zone. Angular ink-like outer edges retain their cutout authority; coarse halftone now denser in the shadow zones; newsprint grain more pronounced in the white field left of her seated form; slight registration shifts separate her shelf brows from the tilted wall top. The elongated calf's knee joint remains cleanly ordinary, its 3x extension credible as deformation rather than foreshortening, the calf muscle flat in photographic texture against the graphic red field behind. Her weight bears through her ordinary right hip and the heel of her right hand on the ground; the left hand with its crumpled list hangs between her thighs. The composition reads bottom-left to top-right as collapse become monument, the diagonal calf a bridge between claimed origin and manufactured outcome. Exactly 1 East Asian adult fills the image - 1 woman and no men.
